2Chr 36:11 [בֶּן] [עֶשְׂרִ֧ים] [וְאַחַ֛ת] [שָׁנָ֖ה] [צִדְקִיָּ֣הוּ] [בְמָלְכ֑וֹ] [וְאַחַ֤ת] [עֶשְׂרֵה֙] [שָׁנָ֔ה] [מָלַ֖ךְ] [בִּירוּשָׁלִָֽם] | [KJV:] Zedekiah was one and twenty years old when he began to reign, and reigned eleven years in Jerusalem.
2Chr 36:12 [וַיַּ֣עַשׂ] [הָרַ֔ע] [בְּעֵינֵ֖י] [יְהוָ֣ה] [אֱלֹהָ֑יו] [לֹ֣א] [נִכְנַ֗ע] [מִלְִּנֵ֛י] [יִרְמְיָ֥הוּ] [הַנָּבִ֖יא] [מִִּ֥י] [יְהוָֽה] | [KJV:] And he did that which was evil in the sight of the Lord his God, and humbled not himself before Jeremiah the prophet speaking from the mouth of the Lord.
2Chr 36:13 [וְ֠גַם] [בַּמֶּ֤לֶךְ] [נְבֽוּכַדְנֶאצַּר֙] [מָרָ֔ד] [אֲשֶׁ֥ר] [הִשְׁבִּיע֖וֹ] [בֵּֽאלֹהִ֑ים] [וַיֶּ֤קֶשׁ] [with] [עָרְּוֹ֙] [וַיְאַמֵּ֣ץ] [with] [לְבָב֔וֹ] [מִשּׁ֕וּב] to/toward [יְהוָ֖ה] [אֱלֹהֵ֥י] [יִשְׂרָאֵֽל] | [KJV:] And he also rebelled against king Nebuchadnezzar, who had made him swear by God: but he stiffened his neck, and hardened his heart from turning unto the Lord God of Israel.
2Chr 36:14 [גַּ֠ם] [כָּל] [שָׂרֵ֨י] [הַכֹּהֲנִ֤ים] [וְהָעָם֙] [הִרְבּ֣וּ] [לִמְעָול] [מַ֔עַל‪n,q‬‏] [כְּכֹ֖ל] [תֹּעֲב֣וֹת] [הַגּוֹיִ֑ם] [וַֽיְטַמְּאוּ֙] [with] [בֵּ֣ית] [יְהוָ֔ה] [אֲשֶׁ֥ר] [הִקְדִּ֖ישׁ] [בִּירוּשָׁלִָֽם] | [KJV:] Moreover all the chief of the priests, and the people, transgressed very much after all the abominations of the heathen; and polluted the house of the Lord which he had hallowed in Jerusalem.
2Chr 36:15 [וַיִּשְׁלַ֡ח] [יְהוָה֩] [אֱלֹהֵ֨י] [אֲבוֹתֵיהֶ֧ם] [עֲלֵיהֶ֛ם] [בְּיַ֥ד] [מַלְאָכָ֖יו] [הַשְׁכֵּ֣ם] [וְשָׁל֑וֹחַ] [כִּֽי] [חָמַ֥ל] upon [עַמּ֖וֹ] and upon [מְעוֹנֽוֹ] | [KJV:] And the Lord God of their fathers sent to them by his messengers, rising up betimes, and sending; because he had compassion on his people, and on his dwelling place:
2Chr 36:16 [וַיִּֽהְי֤וּ] [מַלְעִבִים֙] [בְּמַלְאֲכֵ֣י] [הָאֱלֹהִ֔ים] [וּבוֹזִ֣ים] [דְּבָרָ֔יו] [וּמִֽתַּעְתְּעִ֖ים] [בִּנְבִאָ֑יו] [עַ֠ד] [עֲל֧וֹת] [חֲמַת] [יְהוָ֛ה] [בְּעַמּ֖וֹ] until [לְאֵ֥ין] [מַרְֵּֽא] | [KJV:] But they mocked the messengers of God, and despised his words, and misused his prophets, until the wrath of the Lord arose against his people, till there was no remedy.
2Chr 36:17 [וַיַּ֨עַל] [עֲלֵיהֶ֜ם] [with] [מֶ֣לֶךְ] [כשדיים] [כַּשְׂדִּ֗ים] [וַיַּהֲרֹ֨ג] [בַּחוּרֵיהֶ֤ם] [בַּחֶ֙רֶב֙] [בְּבֵ֣ית] [מִקְדָּשָׁ֔ם] [וְלֹ֥א] [חָמַ֛ל] upon [בָּח֥וּר] [וּבְתוּלָ֖ה] [זָקֵ֣ן] [וְיָשֵׁ֑שׁ] [הַכֹּ֖ל] [נָתַ֥ן] [בְּיָדֽוֹ] | [KJV:] Therefore he brought upon them the king of the Chaldees, who slew their young men with the sword in the house of their sanctuary, and had no compassion upon young man or maiden, old man, or him that stooped for age: he gave them all into his hand.
2Chr 36:18 [וְ֠כֹל] [כְּלֵ֞י] [בֵּ֤ית] [הָאֱלֹהִים֙] [הַגְּדֹלִ֣ים] [וְהַקְּטַנִּ֔ים] [וְאֹֽצְרוֹת֙] [בֵּ֣ית] [יְהוָ֔ה] [וְאֹצְר֥וֹת] [הַמֶּ֖לֶךְ] [וְשָׂרָ֑יו] [הַכֹּ֖ל] [הֵבִ֥יא] [בָבֶֽל] | [KJV:] And all the vessels of the house of God, great and small, and the treasures of the house of the Lord, and the treasures of the king, and of his princes; all these he brought to Babylon.
2Chr 36:19 [וַֽיִּשְׂרְוּ֙] [with] [בֵּ֣ית] [הָאֱלֹהִ֔ים] [וַֽיְנַתְּצ֔וּ] [אֵ֖ת] [חוֹמַ֣ת] [יְרוּשָׁלִָ֑ם] and all [אַרְמְנוֹתֶ֙יהָ֙] [שָׂרְ֣וּ] [בָאֵ֔שׁ] and all [כְּלֵ֥י] [מַחֲמַדֶּ֖יהָ] [לְהַשְׁחִֽית] | [KJV:] And they burnt the house of God, and brake down the wall of Jerusalem, and burnt all the palaces thereof with fire, and destroyed all the goodly vessels thereof.
2Chr 36:20 [וַיֶּ֛גֶל] [הַשְּׁאֵרִ֥ית] from [הַחֶ֖רֶב] to/toward [בָּבֶ֑ל] [וַֽיִּהְיוּ] [ל֤וֹ] [וּלְבָנָיו֙] [לַעֲבָדִ֔ים] until [מְלֹ֖ךְ] [מַלְכ֥וּת] [ָּרָֽ] | [KJV:] And them that had escaped from the sword carried he away to Babylon; where they were servants to him and his sons until the reign of the kingdom of Persia:
2Chr 36:21 [לְמַלֹּ֤אות] [דְּבַר] [יְהוָה֙] [בְִּ֣י] [יִרְמְיָ֔הוּ] until [רָצְתָ֥ה] [הָאָ֖רֶץ] [with] [שַׁבְּתוֹתֶ֑יהָ] [כָּל] [יְמֵ֤י] [הָשַּׁמָּה֙] [שָׁבָ֔תָה] [לְמַלֹּ֖אות] [שִׁבְעִ֥ים] [שָׁנָֽה] | [KJV:] To fulfil the word of the Lord by the mouth of Jeremiah, until the land had enjoyed her sabbaths: for as long as she lay desolate she kept sabbath, to fulfil threescore and ten years.
2Chr 36:22 [וּבִשְׁנַ֣ת] [אַחַ֗ת] [לְכ֙וֹרֶשׁ֙] [מֶ֣לֶךְ] [ָּרַ֔] [לִכְל֥וֹת] [דְּבַר] [יְהוָ֖ה] [בְִּ֣י] [יִרְמְיָ֑הוּ] [הֵעִ֣יר] [יְהוָ֗ה] [with] [ר֙וּחַ֙] [כּ֣וֹרֶשׁ] [מֶֽלֶךְ] [ָּרַ֔] [וַיַּֽעֲבֶר] [קוֹל֙] [בְּכָל] [מַלְכוּת֔וֹ] [וְגַם] [בְּמִכְתָּ֖ב] [לֵאמֹֽר] | [KJV:] Now in the first year of Cyrus king of Persia, that the word of the Lord spoken by the mouth of Jeremiah might be accomplished, the Lord stirred up the spirit of Cyrus king of Persia, that he made a proclamation throughout all his kingdom, and put it also in writing, saying,
2Chr 36:23 [כֹּה] [אָמַ֞ר] [כּ֣וֹרֶשׁ] [׀] [מֶ֣לֶךְ] [ָּרַ֗] [כָּל] [מַמְלְכ֤וֹת] [הָאָ֙רֶץ֙] [נָ֣תַן] [לִ֗י] [יְהוָה֙] [אֱלֹהֵ֣י] [הַשָּׁמַ֔יִם] [וְהֽוּא] [ָקַ֤ד] [עָלַי֙] [לִבְנֽוֹת] [ל֣וֹ] [בַ֔יִת] [בִּירוּשָׁלִַ֖ם] [אֲשֶׁ֣ר] [בִּֽיהוּדָ֑ה] [מִֽי] [בָכֶ֣ם] [מִכָּל] [עַמּ֗וֹ] [יְהוָ֧ה] [אֱלֹהָ֛יו] [עִמּ֖וֹ] [וְיָֽעַל] | [KJV:] Thus saith Cyrus king of Persia, All the kingdoms of the earth hath the Lord God of heaven given me; and he hath charged me to build him an house in Jerusalem, which is in Judah. Who is there among you of all his people? The Lord his God be with him, and let him go up.
